# Calibration of the Quest content (v1.1.4-S9, DL-162)

Run 2026-10-02T17:33:14.162Z on AMD RYZEN AI MAX+ 395 w/ Radeon 8060S (32 cores), Node v26.9.0, in 102.4 s, by `HEROESIM_TUNE=0.44 HEROESIM_FLAT=0.4 HEROESIM_SEEDS=10000 npm run harness -- calibrate`.
Content version 25; the drafts in `docs/quests/drafts/` in place of `hero-traits.json`, `quest-templates.json`, `encounters.json`, `synergies.json`, `items.json`.
Heroes: 300 drawn by the Heroes Generator for each of Levels 1, 5, 10, raised by the engine's growth, unattached, at the Morale baseline (steady, form 1), with no gear and no Regard between them (teamwork 1).
Every rate is the chance estimate's (v1.1.4-S5): "if no one falls", carrying nothing.
The difficulty factors were tuned first (`tunedFactors`): each Encounter so that random Parties of 3 at Level 5 succeed at it with a chance of 0.44 at difficulty 60, each of its Checks as hard on its own; each Finale at the middle of its template's difficulties, with Parties of a size the template allows (`aimOf`). 40% of each target at the aim is a flat term, the rest the factor times the difficulty.

## The Quest target, as the estimate sees it

For each template and Level, 48 Quests drawn by the Quest Generator, each met by the best Party (`bestParty`, the Board's "Your best Party" and the harness's policy's Party) of a Roster of 8 Heroes drawn from the Level's pool, redrawn until it can staff the Quest. The target (DL-83 item 4): the player's Quests fail 20 to 30%, and are strong successes at most 50%. These rates assume no one falls; in play, Injuries, Deaths and Morale lower the later Encounters' chances, so failures run higher.

| Level | Quests | Fail, all thirds | Strong, all thirds | Fail, the hardest third | Strong, the hardest third |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| 1 | 576 | 16% | 51% | 28% | 32% |
| 5 | 576 | 12% | 56% | 18% | 41% |
| 10 | 576 | 1% | 80% | 2% | 66% |

## The coverage grid

Each Hero Stat's Checks by kind: the Checks whose score adds the Stat into its value, not those that only choose or filter by it. The gate: at least 3 Checks across at least 2 kinds. 20 of 20 Stats meet it.

## The estimate against the resolver

24 samples, half an Encounter met alone by a random Party, half a template's Quest met by its best Party, each resolved for 10000 seeds with no one falling; 284 chances compared. The widest gap is 1.20 points (2.41 standard errors), at lay-the-restless-dead at difficulty 78, Level 5, 2 Heroes: the Quest success, estimated 49%, resolved 50%. The widest gap of a chance from 1% to 99% is 2.56 standard errors.

## The templates

For each Level and third of the template's difficulty range: the best Party's chance of each Quest band (fail · success · strong), its mean size, and the positive Earned Traits a Quest draws (each Triumph's `trait` chance, before the strong success that grants it); then random Parties of each size the template allows, each meeting its requirements, 12 a Quest.

## The Encounters

For each Level and Party size, 120 Parties drawn at random from the pool, each estimated alone on a Quest of the one Encounter at each difficulty. Each cell is Triumph · Success · Setback · Failure, in whole percentages. Each Check's table follows its Encounter's.
